To address this, we present ForeSightGuide, an anticipatory assistive guidance framework that couples semantic scene understanding with predictive hazard assessment. Unlike reactive systems, ForeSightGuide leverages the reasoning capabilities of VLMs to anticipate obstacle motion, effectively filtering out non-threatening objects to provide concise, actionable guidance. To validate our approach, we introduce a novel dataset captured in complex, dynamic real-world traffic scenes, designed to benchmark predictive capabilities. Extensive experiments on both public benchmarks and our proposed dataset demonstrate that ForeSightGuide achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, it significantly mitigates information overload by reducing redundant alerts to 0.299 per guidance output while maintaining a low missed-hazard rate of 0.112, proving its efficacy for safe walking assistance.

</details>


### [69] [GrabVG: Graph-Attentive Binding for Visual Grounding in UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18996)
*Chaowei Wang,Yan Di,Jingjun Sun,Baozhe Liu,Jiaxu Tian,Yuheng Li,Guangqian Guo,Shan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GrabVG是一个受人类视觉搜索启发的UAV图像视觉定位框架，将定位显式分解为前注意假设搜索和图注意特征绑定两个阶段，通过在稀疏图上进行语言引导的实例内视觉线索与实例间拓扑关系的联合绑定与传播，在AerialVG和AerialSense数据集上分别达到67.31%和80.34%的Acc@0.5，超越基线10.55和8.76个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV图像中的视觉定位面临两大核心挑战：一是图像中大量小型、密集分布且视觉相似的目标造成高视觉冗余；二是重复的局部配置导致强拓扑歧义。现有方法主要依赖视觉-语言特征对齐或密集上下文交互，但难以区分细粒度的实例间差异，也难以有效利用空间拓扑结构，在高度拥挤场景下定位不准确。

Method: GrabVG将视觉定位显式分解为两个顺序阶段：(1) 前注意假设搜索——通过蒸馏引导的候选框归纳和文本感知假设过滤生成紧凑可靠的物体假设集，减少背景干扰和语义不匹配；(2) 图注意特征绑定——将筛选后的假设组织成稀疏图，通过图注意力机制联合绑定并传播语言引导的实例内视觉线索和实例间拓扑关系，实现高效空间推理与精准目标定位。

Result: 在AerialVG和AerialSense数据集上，GrabVG分别达到67.31%和80.34%的Acc@0.5，超出对应基线10.55和8.76个百分点，同时实现了良好的精度-速度权衡。

Conclusion: 通过模拟人类视觉搜索的双阶段认知机制，GrabVG有效解决了UAV场景中高视觉冗余与拓扑歧义问题，在保持高效推理的同时显著提升了密集拥挤场景下的视觉定位精度，验证了前注意搜索与图注意绑定范式在UAV视觉定位中的有效性。

Abstract: Visual grounding in Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) imagery aims to localize a target object in complex bird's-eye-view scenes according to a natural language description. However, the abundance of small, densely distributed, and visually similar objects creates high visual redundancy, while repetitive local configurations give rise to strong topological ambiguity. Existing approaches mainly focus on visual--language feature alignment or dense contextual interaction, yet they struggle to distinguish subtle inter-instance differences and effectively exploit spatial topological structures, leading to inaccurate grounding in highly crowded scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose $\textbf{GrabVG}$, a novel visual grounding framework inspired by human visual search. GrabVG explicitly decomposes grounding into two sequential stages: $\textit{preattentive hypothesis search}$ and $\textit{graph-attentive feature binding}$. Specifically, we first generate a compact set of reliable object hypotheses through distillation-guided proposal induction and text-aware hypothesis filtering, substantially reducing background distractions and semantic mismatches. These hypotheses are then organized into a sparse graph, where language-guided intra-instance visual cues and inter-instance topological relationships are jointly bound and propagated via graph attention, enabling efficient spatial reasoning and accurate target localization. Extensive experiments on AerialVG and AerialSense show that GrabVG achieves a favorable accuracy--speed trade-off, reaching 67.31$\%$ and 80.34$\%$ Acc@0.5 and outperforming the corresponding baselines by 10.55 and 8.76 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [70] [Mise-en-Scène: Implicit Layout Emergence in Diffusion Transformers for Human-AI Design Co-Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19000)
*Zipeng Xu,Ryan Murdock,Umberto Michieli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mise-en-Scène，一个两阶段图形设计合成框架。第一阶段利用经过小规模knockout-selected LoRA适配的预训练图像编辑扩散Transformer，让布局与渲染画布联合隐式涌现；第二阶段通过确定性的match-and-place步骤将原始高分辨率资产放置到草稿位置，保证资产保真度并生成可编辑的分层设计。在PrismLayersPlus基准上，该方法在感知质量上大幅超越LLM布局规划器和专用布局Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将布局预测（基于语言模型生成显式边界框坐标）与视觉合成分离，导致生成结果僵硬、缩放失当，且无法在空间规划中融入视觉信息。作者质疑这一范式，探索能否让布局在预训练图像编辑扩散Transformer内部隐式涌现，从而实现布局与视觉的联合生成。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段，使用knockout-selected LoRA适配预训练扩散Transformer，无需专用条件机制，使元素的排列与渲染画布联合生成完整设计草稿；第二阶段，采用确定性match-and-place步骤，将原始高分辨率资产精确移动到草稿对应的位置，既保证资产完全保真，又输出可继续编辑的分层设计而非扁平图像。

Result: 在大规模PrismLayersPlus基准上，Mise-en-Scène生成的设计在感知质量上最接近ground truth，大幅优于基于LLM的布局规划器和专用布局Transformer；match-and-place阶段进一步弥合了与ground-truth合成图像之间的保真度差距。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，仅通过最少的预训练扩散Transformer适配即可实现高质量的多元素图形设计合成，无需专门的条件建模机制，布局可以在扩散模型内部隐式涌现，并通过确定性后处理保证资产保真。这为图形设计合成提供了一种简洁而有效的替代范式。

Abstract: Automating graphic design synthesis from user-provided elements requires both a coherent overall composition and the exact preservation of each asset. Existing methods predict a layout as explicit bounding-box coordinates with a language model and then paste the assets into it, which separates spatial planning from visual synthesis and tends to produce rigid, mis-scaled compositions. We instead ask whether the layout can emerge implicitly inside a pretrained image-editing diffusion transformer. We present Mise-en-Scène, a two-stage framework. In the first stage, a diffusion transformer adapted with a small, knockout-selected LoRA drafts a complete design in which the arrangement of the elements emerges jointly with the rendered canvas. In the second stage, a deterministic match-and-place step moves the original high-resolution assets to the drafted positions, which guarantees exact asset fidelity and yields an editable, layered design that a designer can keep refining rather than a flat image. Notably, a minimal adaptation of the pretrained transformer already suffices, without the specialized conditioning machinery commonly introduced for multi-element generation. On the large-scale PrismLayersPlus benchmark, the designs produced by Mise-en-Scène are the closest to the ground truth in perceived quality among all compared methods, by a wide margin over both an LLM layout planner and a specialized layout transformer, while our match-and-place stage bridges the remaining fidelity gap to the ground-truth composites.

</details>


### [71] [One-Stage Object Detectors in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19014)
*Jonel Roman,Ryan Sirjue,Peter Nguyen,Daniel Krutky,Juan Jesus,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于自动驾驶中单阶段目标检测器的综合性综述，系统梳理了从YOLOv1到YOLOv10等主要检测架构的演进，并从设计选择、特征融合策略、损失函数、部署权衡和基准性能等维度进行比较分析，同时总结了常用数据集、评估指标及开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统依赖快速可靠的目标检测来实时识别周围车辆、行人、骑行者、交通标志等道路目标。考虑到双阶段检测器在实时性上的局限，以及单阶段检测器在速度与精度之间的重要权衡，有必要对该类检测器进行系统性的梳理与分析，以揭示其演进规律和当前瓶颈。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法，回顾了YOLOv1、SSD、RetinaNet、EfficientDet等经典单阶段检测器，以及FCOS、CenterNet等无锚框检测器和YOLOv10等近期实时模型，围绕设计选择、特征融合策略、损失函数、部署权衡与基准性能五个维度进行对比分析，并总结了自动驾驶常用数据集和评估指标。

Result: 该综述揭示了单阶段检测器在速度、精度、效率和鲁棒性之间寻求平衡的总体趋势，指出从有锚框到无锚框、从固定架构到尺度高效设计的演进路径，同时强调了基准测试结果与真实世界自动驾驶可靠性能之间仍存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 单阶段检测器已在自动驾驶感知中占据重要地位，但仍需弥合基准性能与实际部署之间的鸿沟，未来的研究方向包括提升鲁棒性、应对复杂场景以及在边缘设备上的高效部署等。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles depend on fast and reliable perception systems to detect surrounding vehicles, pedestrians, cyclists, traffic signs, and other road objects in real time. This paper presents a comprehensive survey and analysis of one-stage object detectors for autonomous driving rather than an implementation of a new detection system. The survey reviews the evolution of major one-stage detectors, including YOLOv1, SSD, RetinaNet, EfficientDet, anchor-free detectors such as FCOS and CenterNet, and recent real-time models such as YOLOv10. It compares these architectures through their design choices, feature-fusion strategies, loss functions, deployment trade-offs, and reported benchmark performance. The paper also summarizes commonly used autonomous-driving datasets, evaluation metrics, open challenges, and future research directions. Overall, this survey highlights how one-stage detectors balance speed, accuracy, efficiency, and robustness, while also emphasizing the remaining gap between benchmark results and dependable real-world autonomous-driving performance.

</details>


### [72] [Orthogonal Polynomial Approximation for Matrix Log Normalization in Global Covariance Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19021)
*Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Raihan Khan,Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Pietro Liò,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需特征分解的矩阵对数（logarithm）归一化方法用于全局协方差池化（GCP）。通过均值特征值预归一化将谱中心化到1附近，并用多项式（推荐8阶Chebyshev展开）近似矩阵对数，前后向传播都只需GEMM运算，避免了传统谱分解方法中1/(λi-λj)项的数值不稳定问题。在三个细粒度基准和ImageNet-1k上，该方法比谱对数及平方根近似更快更准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局协方差池化（GCP）通过捕获二阶特征统计有效提升细粒度识别性能，但协方差矩阵位于SPD流形上，需归一化后才能用于欧氏分类器。矩阵对数（MLN-COV）是忠实选择，但其基于特征分解的梯度数值不稳定，因此实践中被平方根近似取代。作者欲证明不稳定性源于谱计算方式而非对数本身。

Method: 1) 提出均值特征值预归一化（mean-eigenvalue pre-normalization），将谱中心移到1附近远离log的奇异点，并用标量后补偿在闭式下恢复log(A)的奇异部分；2) 推荐8阶Chebyshev多项式展开，通过三项矩阵递推在前向计算，匹配的反向递推用于反向传播，全过程仅涉及GEMM；3) 对比Legendre、Laguerre、Taylor和Padé展开作为对照，隔离基函数和目标函数各自的作用。

Result: 在三个细粒度基准和ImageNet-1k上：分解自由的对数比谱对数及平方根近似更快且更准确；在匹配基数和阶数下，log目标优于平方根目标，证实性能提升来自忠实的黎曼映射而非更优的多项式族。

Conclusion: 矩阵对数在GCP中的数值不稳定是特征分解计算的产物，而非对数本身的固有缺陷。通过多项式近似配合均值特征值预归一化，可稳定高效地实现忠实于SPD流形几何的归一化，在精确性和计算效率上均优于平方根近似。

Abstract: Global Covariance Pooling (GCP) improves deep networks by capturing second-order feature statistics, and is especially effective for fine-grained recognition. Because covariance matrices live on the Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) manifold, a normalization step is required before the Euclidean classifier. The faithful choice is the matrix logarithm (MLN-COV), which maps the SPD manifold to its tangent space; in practice it was abandoned in favour of the matrix square root because its eigendecomposition-based gradient is numerically unstable. We show that this instability is an artifact of computing the logarithm spectrally, not of the logarithm itself. Approximating the logarithm with finite polynomials in the covariance matrix removes the eigendecomposition from both passes: every operation becomes a General Matrix Multiplication (GEMM), the gradient stays bounded on the spectral support of the pre-normalized covariance, and the unstable 1/(lambda_i-lambda_j) term never appears. The key ingredient is a mean-eigenvalue pre-normalization that centres the spectrum near 1, away from the singularity of log, with a scalar post-compensation that returns the singular part of log(A) in closed form. Our recommended normalizer is a degree-8 Chebyshev expansion evaluated by a three-term matrix recurrence, with a matching reverse recurrence for the backward pass; Legendre, Laguerre, Taylor and Pade expansions are studied as controls that isolate the roles of the basis and of the target function. On three fine-grained benchmarks and ImageNet-1k the decomposition-free logarithm is both faster and more accurate than the spectral logarithm and than the square-root approximations it replaces, and at matched basis and degree the log target beats the square-root target, confirming that the gain comes from the faithful Riemannian map rather than from a better polynomial family.

</details>


### [73] [Counterfactual Contrastive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19032)
*Yunlong He,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对比分析（Contrastive Analysis）的无需分类器的视觉反事实解释方法，通过直接在数据分布层面（而非决策边界）分离并交换两个类别的显著生成因子来生成反事实图像，从而规避分类器偏差问题。方法基于StyleGAN2的特征空间F和适配的损失函数，支持每个数据集中存在多个显著因子，在三个医学影像数据集上取得优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉反事实解释（VCE）方法本质上依赖于分类器，因此容易受到分类器偏差（如捷径特征敏感性、校准误差）的影响。作者希望提出一种不依赖分类器的方案，直接从数据分布层面进行反事实生成，使解释结果对分类器偏差具有鲁棒性。

Method: 1）基于对比分析（CA）框架，将两个类别数据集（如健康和患者）的生成因子解耦为公共因子和各自数据集的显著因子；2）通过仅交换显著因子生成反事实图像；3）利用StyleGAN2的高质量合成能力和结构良好的潜在空间，使用特征空间F替代常规的W空间以提升细节保留；4）扩展传统的CA方法，允许每个数据集存在多个显著因子，并设计了相应的损失函数。

Result: 在三个医学影像数据集上进行评估，所提方法在反事实生成质量上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过从数据分布而非决策边界出发，该方法提供了模型无关的视觉反事实解释，对分类器偏差不敏感，并展示了在医学影像解释领域的实用性。

Abstract: Visual Counterfactual Explanations (VCEs) aim to explain image classifiers by generating minimally edited and realistic versions of an input image that change the classifier's prediction. Existing VCE methods are inherently classifier-dependent and therefore susceptible to classifier biases and failure modes, such as sensitivity to shortcut features and calibration errors. In this paper, we propose a classifier-free approach for visual counterfactual generation based on Contrastive Analysis (CA). Given two datasets corresponding to different classes (e.g., healthy and patients), we disentangle the generative factors that are common across the two datasets from those that are salient to each dataset, and generate counterfactual images by swapping only the salient factors. By operating directly on data distributions rather than decision boundaries, our method provides model-agnostic VCEs that are less sensitive to classifier biases. Our approach leverages the high-quality synthesis and well-structured latent space of StyleGAN2. We use the feature space F, instead than the usual W-space, to improve detail preservation. Unlike conventional CA approaches, which typically assume salient factors in only one dataset, we introduce an adapted framework and loss functions for VCE that allow multiple salient factors in each dataset. We evaluate our method on three medical imaging datasets and demonstrate superior counterfactual generation quality compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [74] [USR-Drive: Unified Driving Scene Representation via Joint Denoising of 3D Gaussians and Boxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19036)
*Li-Heng Chen,Haokai Pang,Chengye Su,Jiarun Liu,Qifeng Chen,Ziqian Ni,Jianxin Huang,Shi-Sheng Huang,Hongbo Fu,Sheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: USR-Drive是一个统一的条件生成框架，通过将稠密3D高斯原语和稀疏3D边界框表示为对齐的潜在token流，并在共享的度量时空坐标系中联合去噪，同时解决动态场景重建和实例级3D检测两个任务，实现两者的相互约束与增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶空间表征学习方法通常将动态重建和实例级感知视为两个独立任务，导致动态重建约束不足、3D检测缺乏几何依据。尽管二者共享估计底层3D世界状态的目标，但缺乏统一的表征框架来协同这两个互补的信息层级。

Method: USR-Drive将稠密3D高斯原语和稀疏3D边界框编码为两个对齐的潜在token流，使用统一的多模态扩散Transformer联合去噪。通过统一位置编码（UPE）将异构token对齐到共享的度量时空坐标系中，将边界框视为与几何相互约束的状态变量而非外部条件，实现几何为检测提供度量证据、检测为几何提供实例级结构先验的相互增强。

Result: 在nuScenes和VKitti数据集上，USR-Drive在动态重建和3D检测两个任务上均取得了最先进（SOTA）的结果。

Conclusion: 通过统一的表征和条件生成框架，动态几何重建与实例级感知可以相互增益：几何提供稠密度量证据提升检测精度，检测提供实例级结构先验增强几何重建的空间一致性与歧义消除能力，验证了统一建模的有效性。

Abstract: Spatial representation learning for autonomous driving aims to map raw visual signals into structured 3D scene representations, where object-centric bounding boxes and rendering-oriented 3D primitives (\eg, 3D Gaussians) serve as two distinct yet highly complementary levels for scene understanding. Existing methods typically treat dynamic reconstruction and instance-level perception as separate tasks, despite their shared goal of estimating the underlying 3D world state. As a result, dynamic reconstruction is under-constrained while 3D detection lacks geometric grounding. To address this gap, we propose USR-Drive, a unified conditional generative framework that, given only posed multi-view driving videos, jointly recovers dense dynamic geometry and instance-level object layouts within a shared scene representation. Specifically, USR-Drive represents dense Gaussian primitives and sparse 3D bounding boxes as two aligned latent token streams and jointly denoises them with a unified multi-modal diffusion Transformer. Unlike prior paradigms that use boxes as external conditions or predict them with detached modules, USR-Drive treats them as mutually constrained state variables with a Unified Positional Encoding (UPE) that aligns heterogeneous tokens within a shared metric spatiotemporal coordinate. Via such unified representation and generative framework, the two modalities reinforce each other: geometry supplies dense metric evidence for box prediction, while boxes provide instance-level structural priors that help preserve spatial consistency and reduce ambiguity in sequential 3D geometric representation. Our approach successfully delivers state-of-the-art results for both dynamic reconstruction and 3D detection on the nuScenes and VKitti datasets.

</details>


### [75] [Generalized Audio-Driven Synthesis of Precise Drummer Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19055)
*Álvaro G. Iñesta,Mattia Ryffel,Amit H. Bermano,Robert W. Sumner,Martin Guay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生成式扩散框架的音乐驱动击鼓动画生成方法，通过双目标损失函数解耦骨骼完整性与鼓槌精度，实现对非整理音频的泛化，并引入两个新指标（impact-to-target distance和audio-motion correlation）来量化空间精度与时间对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐驱动的角色动画在娱乐和互动教育中有广阔应用，但合成逼真的鼓击动画面临高加速度动力学与极端时空精度之间的内在矛盾。现有方法依赖动作匹配或MIDI输入，难以泛化到多样化的真实音频，且缺乏能区分精确击鼓与噪声动作的标准化评估指标。

Method: 提出一个生成式扩散框架，包含双目标损失函数，分别优化骨骼完整性与鼓槌精度，实现在保持自然身体动态的同时达到厘米级鼓槌精度。利用自建数据集与数据增强策略，使模型能够泛化到未经整理的真实世界音频。同时提出两个新评估指标：impact-to-target distance用于量化空间精度，audio-motion correlation score用于评估时间对齐。

Result: 定量分析与用户研究均表明，该系统生成的高质量鼓击动作往往与真实表演难以区分。

Conclusion: 该扩散框架成功解决了音乐驱动击鼓动画中精度与自然度之间的权衡问题，并通过新提出的评估指标建立了更严格的性能评价标准，为音乐驱动角色动画领域提供了有效且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Music-driven character animation enables and enhances transformative applications in entertainment and interactive education. However, synthesizing realistic drumming motion from audio remains challenging due to the inherent tension between high-acceleration dynamics and the need for extreme spatial-temporal precision. Existing approaches, often reliant on motion matching or MIDI input, struggle with generalizing to diverse real-world audio. Moreover, the field lacks standardized evaluation metrics capable of distinguishing precise drumming from noisy motion. In this paper, we introduce a generative diffusion framework featuring a dual-objective loss function that decouples skeletal integrity from drumstick precision, thus enabling centimeter-level stick precision without sacrificing natural body dynamics. Additionally, leveraging our own dataset and data augmentation strategy, the model generalizes to non-curated, in-the-wild audio. To rigorously evaluate performance, we propose two novel metrics: an impact-to-target distance to quantify spatial precision and an audio-motion correlation score to assess temporal alignment. Our quantitative analysis and user studies demonstrate that our system generates high-quality motion that is often indistinguishable from ground-truth performances.

</details>


### [76] [When Two Tracers Disagree: An Investigation of Multimodal Fusion for Clinical PET/CT Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19063)
*Jack A. Johnson,Bartłomiej W. Papież*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了PSMA与FDG PET/CT多模态融合策略在前列腺癌全身病灶分割中的效果。基于DEEP-PSMA Challenge公开数据集，训练了单示踪剂的3D nnU-Net基线，并比较了早期融合（单编码器单/双解码器）与中期融合（双编码器交叉注意力DECA-UNet）。结果显示单示踪剂基线表现强劲（PSMA Dice=0.93，FDG=0.81），而融合模型未能在两个示踪剂上同时超越基线，FDG性能普遍下降，表明当前融合架构难以保真示踪剂特异性表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSMA和FDG PET/CT在前列腺癌中提供互补的生物学信息，联合使用可捕获单独使用任一示踪剂可能遗漏的异质性肿瘤表型。然而，目前缺乏有效的深度学习架构来融合这两种模态，本研究旨在系统评估不同多模态融合策略在全身病灶分割中的表现。

Method: 使用DEEP-PSMA Challenge公开数据集，训练示踪剂特异的3D nnU-Net基线；对比两种融合策略：(i)早期融合——单编码器配单解码器（OEOD）或双解码器（OETD）；(ii)中期融合——双编码器交叉注意力U-Net（DECA-UNet），并评估各模型在全身PET/CT病灶分割中的Dice系数。

Result: 单示踪剂基线表现强劲（PSMA Dice=0.93；FDG=0.81）。融合结果参差不齐：OEOD在非示踪剂特异的较简单任务上达到联合Dice 0.90；示踪剂特异融合模型中，OETD达到PSMA/FDG=0.69/0.64，DECA-UNet达到0.76/0.57。融合虽常能提供合理的PSMA分割，但FDG性能下降，且没有任何策略能持续超越单示踪剂基线。

Conclusion: 在当前评估设置下，示踪剂特异性模型仍是更强的基线方案；临床上有价值的多模态融合增益很可能需要能更好保持示踪剂特异性表征的架构设计。

Abstract: PSMA and FDG PET/CT visualise complementary biological information in prostate cancer. Combining both tracers could capture heterogeneous tumour phenotypes that may be missed by either alone, yet there is no consensus on effective deep learning architectures for fusing these modalities. We evaluated multimodal image-fusion strategies for automatic whole-body PET/CT lesion segmentation to estimate total tumour burden. Using the public DEEP-PSMA Challenge dataset, we trained tracer-specific 3D nnU-Net baselines and compared (i) early fusion with a single encoder and one decoder (OEOD) or two decoders (OETD), and (ii) intermediate fusion via a dual-encoder cross-attention U-Net (DECA-UNet). Tracer-specific baselines performed strongly (PSMA Dice = 0.93; FDG = 0.81). Fusion yielded mixed results: OEOD produced a combined Dice of 0.90 (on an easier, non-tracer-specific task), whilst the tracer-specific fusion models reached PSMA/FDG = 0.69/0.64 (OETD) and 0.76/0.57 (DECA-UNet). Whilst fusion often provided reasonable PSMA segmentation, FDG performance degraded and no strategy consistently exceeded the single-tracer baselines. Under the evaluated setting, tracer-specific models remain the stronger baseline; clinically useful gains from multimodal fusion will likely require architectures that better preserve tracer specific representations. Our code is available at: https://github.com/JackJ3636/DEEP_PSMA_code

</details>


### [77] [GS-VLA: Plug-and-Play Viewpoint Canonicalization for Frozen VLA Policies via Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19066)
*Yechan Park,HyunJin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GS-VLA，一个即插即用的轻量级框架，通过3D高斯新视角合成技术将VLA策略输入图像归一化到训练时的视角，在不重训练策略的前提下显著提升对相机视角偏移的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略隐式假设训练与部署时的相机配置一致，实际中相机支架哪怕是微小位移也会导致成功率从约90%骤降至约10%。已有的大规模微调或生成式数据增强方法计算开销大且有灾难性遗忘风险，亟需一种轻量、无需重训练的视角鲁棒化方案。

Method: 将视角偏移问题重新表述为局部新视角合成问题。在\"局部性假设\"（相机扰动相对于工作空间保持在小范围内）下，视角归一化退化为一个与场景和策略无关的去遮挡任务。作者实现了一个仅含4M参数的3D高斯canonicalizer模块，将其前置到冻结的VLA策略之前，实现观测空间的视角归一化。

Result: 在不修改任何策略权重的前提下，GS-VLA在三个正交维度上均提升了性能：（1）不同策略架构；（2）未见过的任务套件；（3）不同扰动尺度。实验表明一个轻量视觉模块可以恢复大部分因视角偏移而损失的性能。

Conclusion: 轻量化的视觉前端模块能够在无需策略重训练的情况下有效弥补VLA策略在视角偏移下大幅退化的性能，为VLA策略的实际部署提供了一种高效、即插即用的鲁棒性增强手段。

Abstract: This paper proposes a lightweight, plug-and-play framework that improves robustness to viewpoint shifts in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies without policy retraining. To our knowledge, this is the first approach to directly leverage 3D Gaussian-based novel-view synthesis for observation-space adaptation in VLA policies. Current VLA performance relies on the implicit assumption that training and deployment camera configurations are identical. Our experiments show that even a small displacement of the camera mount can reduce the success rate on the LIBERO benchmark from about 90% to about 10% in the worst case. Prior approaches, such as large-scale fine-tuning or generative data augmentation, are computationally expensive and risk catastrophic forgetting. To address this, viewpoint shifts are reformulated as a localized novel-view synthesis problem. Under a Locality assumption, that camera perturbations remain within a small bounded region relative to the workspace, viewpoint normalization reduces to a scene- and policy-independent disocclusion task. Our work implements this idea with a 4M-parameter 3D-Gaussian canonicalizer prepended to a frozen VLA policy. Without modifying policy weights, GS-VLA improves performance across three orthogonal axes: (1) Policy architectures, (2) Unseen task suites, and (3) Perturbation scales. These results show that a lightweight visual module can recover a large fraction of the performance lost under viewpoint shift, without policy retraining.

</details>


### [78] [ReWEIGH the Evidence: Calibrating Token-Level Ordinal Visual Evidence to Mitigate Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19075)
*Jihae Jeong,Junha Choi,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReWEIGH是一种无需训练的推理时解码干预方法，通过聚合视觉位置的词汇排名证据并与无语义图像的token特定参考比较，对低于参考的候选token施加有界惩罚，显著减少LVLM的幻觉对象提及（最多降低21.3%），同时保持整体性能，额外延迟仅1.33%/token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）经常产生输入图像不支持的幻觉内容。现有方法缺少一种针对候选token的、能衡量图像对该token支持强度的度量标准。虽然模型视觉token状态通过输出头投影可提供逐位置证据，但其概率幅度在视觉位置上不可直接比较，且词汇排名虽为尺度不变但token间存在系统性差异。

Method: ReWEIGH提出在预填充阶段缓存图像证据，将每个视觉位置的词汇排名证据跨位置聚合，并与从未标记（无图像）数据中估计的token特定参考值进行比较。推理时仅对低于参考阈值的候选token施加有界惩罚，属于训练免费的解码干预方法。

Result: 在四个7B骨干模型上，ReWEIGH将幻觉对象提及减少高达21.3%，同时基本保持或提升描述性和通用任务性能。缓存证据后每个token的平均额外延迟仅1.33%，效果可扩展到六个架构家族直至32B参数规模。

Conclusion: ReWEIGH通过利用视觉token状态的排名证据及token特定参考值，提供了一种轻量、高效且可扩展的幻觉抑制方法，在不损失生成质量的前提下显著降低LVLM的幻觉，且具备跨架构和规模的良好泛化性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate, generating content that the input image does not support. Preventing such content during decoding calls for a candidate-specific measure of how strongly the image supports the token under consideration. The model's visual-token states offer a natural source of this evidence because projecting each state through the output head reveals which vocabulary items that position favors. These position-wise readouts cannot be pooled directly because their probability magnitudes are not comparable across visual positions. Vocabulary ranks provide a scale-invariant basis for pooling, but tokens still differ systematically in their typical rank-based evidence. We propose ReWEIGH, a training-free decoding intervention that aggregates these ranks across visual positions and compares each candidate with a token-specific reference estimated from unlabeled images. At inference, ReWEIGH caches the image evidence during prefill and applies a bounded penalty only to candidates that fall below their reference. On four 7B backbones, ReWEIGH reduces hallucinated object mentions by up to 21.3% while largely preserving or improving descriptive and general performance. With evidence cached, the average added latency is 1.33% per token, and the reductions extend across six architecture families to 32B parameters.

</details>


### [79] [Subgroup performance analysis of adaptation strategies for chest X-ray foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19078)
*Dhruv Gupta,Emma A. M. Stanley,Fabio De Sousa Ribeiro,Sujal Desai,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了三种参数高效微调策略（线性头、MLP、注意力池化）应用于冻结的Rad-DINO胸部X光编码器时，对病理分类性能与亚组公平性的影响。结果发现注意力池化性能最佳但最强地编码了受保护属性（特别是种族），然而更强的属性编码并不对应更大的亚组差异，早期网络层对种族编码最弱却产生了最大亚组性能差距，表明公平性影响是任务依赖且不可预测的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型越来越多地被用于医学影像下游任务，但不同的适配策略对亚组公平性的影响尚未被充分理解。理解适配策略与公平性之间的关系对于确保医学AI在种族、性别等亚组间公平至关重要。

Method: 在冻结的Rad-DINO胸部X光编码器上应用三种参数高效适配技术（原始CLS标记上的线性头、MLP、基于多层patch特征的注意力池化模块），使用MIMIC-CXR数据集，在保持患病率、人口统计平衡的测试集上评估八种病理在种族、性别和影像视角亚组中的表现，并探测各适配器对受保护属性的编码强度。

Result: 注意力池化实现了最强的整体判别性能并最强地编码了受保护属性（尤其种族），但更好的整体性能并不一致地减少亚组差异。更强的属性编码并不对应更大的差异：早期网络层对种族编码最弱却产生最大的亚组性能差距。探索不同的注意力池化层组合发现，池化层选择、属性编码强度与亚组公平性之间没有一致关系。

Conclusion: 更丰富、更具表达力的表征可以提高准确性，但公平性影响是任务依赖且不可预测的，必须在每个任务中直接评估，而不能仅从编码强度或整体性能来推断。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly adapted for downstream medical imaging tasks, yet the influence of the chosen adaptation strategy on subgroup fairness remains poorly understood. We investigate how three parameter-efficient adaptation techniques, including linear heads on the raw CLS token, an MLP, and an attention-pooling module over multi-layer patch features, affect both pathology classification performance and subgroup disparities when applied to the frozen Rad-DINO chest X-ray encoder. Using MIMIC-CXR, we evaluate eight pathologies across race, sex, and imaging-view subgroups on a prevalence-preserving, demographically balanced test set, and additionally probe how strongly each adapter encodes protected attributes. We find that attention pooling achieves the strongest overall discriminative performance and encodes attributes, particularly race, most strongly, but that improved overall performance does not consistently reduce subgroup disparities. Notably, stronger attribute encoding did not correspond to larger disparities: early network layers encoded race most weakly yet produced the largest subgroup performance gaps. Exploring different attention-pooling layer combinations further revealed no consistent relationship between the layers pooled, attribute encoding strength, and subgroup fairness. Our results indicate that richer, more expressive representations can improve accuracy while leaving fairness implications task-dependent and unpredictable, which must be assessed directly and per-task rather than inferred from encoding strength or overall performance alone.

</details>


### [80] [SPK: Eliciting Structured Prior Knowledge for Interpretable Out-of-Distribution Detection in Real-Time Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19080)
*Changshun Wu,Weicheng He,Xiaowei Huang,Saddek Bensalem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化先验知识（SPK）框架，通过显式挖掘预训练目标检测器中隐含的潜在先验（包括部分级语义、几何和上下文先验），构建紧凑的五维表示用于OoD检测，在多个架构和基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测器常对训练类别之外的物体产生过自信预测（OoD幻觉）。现有方法要么直接在检测器表示上构造评分函数，要么修改检测器本身来抑制幻觉，但检测器表示中隐含的潜在先验未被显式解码用于OoD检测。作者认为这些先验知识具有未被充分利用的巨大价值。

Method: SPK框架利用分布内数据和幻觉诱导样本作为诊断监督，显式引出检测器决策背后的部分级语义概念；将这些语义先验与几何和上下文先验整合，形成紧凑的五维SPK表示用于OoD检测。

Result: 在多种目标检测器架构和多个OoD基准上的大量实验表明，SPK达到了最先进的OoD检测性能。

Conclusion: 预训练的目标检测器已编码了远比通常被利用的更丰富的潜在知识，这些知识可以被显式引出并组织成紧凑、结构化、可解释的知识空间，用于预测可靠性分析，为提升检测器可靠性提供了一条主动式、可解释的路径。

Abstract: Object detectors often produce over-confident predictions for objects outside their training categories, leading to so-called out-of-distribution (OoD) hallucinations. Existing approaches for detecting or mitigating such hallucinations typically either construct scoring functions directly over learned object detector representations or modify the object detector itself to suppress hallucination emergence. However, the latent priors implicitly encoded in these representations remain largely unexplored and have not been explicitly decoded for OoD detection. To uncover and exploit these latent priors, we propose Structured Prior Knowledge (SPK), a hallucination-oriented framework that explicitly elicits OoD-relevant priors from pretrained object detectors. Specifically, SPK leverages in-distribution data and hallucination-inducing samples as diagnostic supervision to elicit part-level semantic concepts underlying object detector decision-making, rather than using them merely for rejection or object detector adaptation. The elicited semantic priors are further integrated with geometric and contextual priors to form a compact five-dimensional SPK representation for OoD detection. Extensive experiments across diverse object detector architectures and multiple OoD benchmarks demonstrate that SPK achieves state-of-the-art OoD detection. Our findings reveal that pretrained object detectors already encode substantially richer latent knowledge than is typically exploited for OoD detection. More importantly, this knowledge can be explicitly elicited and organized into a compact, structured, and interpretable knowledge space for prediction reliability analysis. This suggests a promising proactive route for improving object detector reliability by explicitly uncovering and leveraging latent priors. Code and data are available at: https://gricad-gitlab.univ-grenoble-alpes.fr/dnn-safety/spk

</details>


### [81] [Detecting Backdoors in Object Detection via Pre-NMS Prediction Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19088)
*Longtian Wang,Zhengyu Zhao,Chenhao Lin,Le Yang,Shiwei Wang,Yuhan Zhi,Xiaofei Xie,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DistScan是一种基于"后门注入会系统性改变模型pre-NMS预测类分布"这一观察的新型后门检测框架，无需权重访问、触发知识或额外训练，通过在干净验证集上聚合中间类预测并与训练类频率比较来检测场景级后门攻击，在MS-COCO和PASCAL VOC上比现有最佳方法平均检测准确率提升27.32个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门检测方法要么依赖触发反转，要么依赖架构特定假设，且在场景级攻击（单个触发器同时导致场景中所有对象异常行为）下无法可靠泛化。安全关键应用中部署的目标检测模型对后门攻击脆弱，需要更通用、有效的检测方法。

Method: DistScan的核心观察是：后门注入会系统性偏移模型在pre-NMS阶段的预测类分布，即使在无触发器的干净输入上也是如此。具体方法为：在干净验证集上聚合模型的中间类预测（pre-NMS），然后将其与训练类频率进行比较，若分布显著偏离则标记模型为被后门攻击的模型。该方法不要求模型权重访问、不需要触发器知识、也不需额外训练。

Result: 在MS-COCO和PASCAL VOC上，覆盖两种架构和三种场景级攻击场景的实验中，DistScan显著优于现有方法，平均检测准确率比最佳适用基线提高27.32个百分点。

Conclusion: DistScan展示了一种简单但此前未被利用的信号（pre-NMS类分布偏移）可以有效检测场景级后门攻击，为安全关键的目标检测模型提供了一种无需额外资源的高效后门检测方案，具有强泛化能力和实用价值。

Abstract: Object detection models deployed in safety-critical applications remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks that cause targeted misbehaviors when a hidden trigger is present. Existing detection methods either rely on trigger inversion or exploit architecture-specific assumptions, and critically, representative existing methods fail to generalize reliably to scene-level attacks, where a single trigger induces anomalous behavior across all objects in the scene simultaneously. We present DistScan, a backdoor detection framework based on a simple but previously unexploited observation: backdoor injection systematically shifts a model's pre-NMS prediction class distribution away from its training class frequencies, even on clean inputs without any trigger present. DistScan aggregates intermediate class predictions over a clean validation set and flags a model as backdoored if the resulting distribution deviates significantly from the training class frequencies, requiring no model weight access, no trigger knowledge, and no additional training. Extensive experiments on MS-COCO and PASCAL VOC across two architectures and three scene-level attack scenarios demonstrate that DistScan substantially outperforms existing methods, improving average detection accuracy over the best-performing applicable baseline by 27.32 percentage points.

</details>


### [82] [Image-Guided Pavement Defect Recognition in GPR Data with novel 3D Deep Learning Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19177)
*Yuandong Pan,Linjun Lu,Mudan Wang,Florian Noichl,Fan Xue,Brian Sheil,Lavindra de Silva,André Borrmann,Ioannis Brilakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合正射RGB影像与3D GPR数据进行大规模标注的数据准备流程，并设计了一种包含残差连接、混合卷积核及双注意力机制的3D CNN架构，用于路面修补和裂缝缺陷的二元分类检测，在真实高速公路数据集上验证了其优于基线模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPR在路面自动化检测中的大规模应用面临两大挑战：一是标注真实世界数据集的稀缺性，二是缺乏专门针对3D GPR数据独特特征设计的深度学习模型。现有方法难以高效生成标注数据，且通用模型未能充分利用3D GPR数据的信息。

Method: 提出一种成本效益高的数据准备流程：利用对齐的RGB路面表面图像作为参考，将表面可见缺陷的标签迁移到对应的3D GPR数据段，实现真实世界数据集的高效大规模标注。同时提出一种专门的3D CNN架构，包含残差连接、混合卷积核尺寸、深度注意力和通道注意力机制，以增强特征表示和缺陷分类能力。

Result: 在运营中高速公路路段采集的真实数据集上，所提出的网络在修补和裂缝缺陷二元分类任务中，在多项评估指标上均优于基线架构。消融研究进一步证实了各设计组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一种可扩展且实用的真实世界数据集生成方法和一种新颖的深度学习框架，为解决GPR大规模自动化路面检测中的数据稀缺和模型适配问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Ground Penetrating Radar (GPR) is a widely adopted non-destructive sensing technology for subsurface inspection in civil and transportation engineering. Despite its potential for pavement condition assessment, the large-scale application of GPR in automated inspection has two key challenges: the scarcity of annotated real-world datasets and the lack of deep learning models designed for the unique characteristics of 3-Dimensional (3D) GPR data. This study addresses these limitations by firstly introducing a cost-effective data preparation pipeline that integrates orthomosaic Red Green Blue (RGB) imagery with 3D GPR scans to generate annotated 3D GPR datasets. The proposed method uses the aligned segments of RGB and GPR data, using pavement surface images as a reference to transfer labels of surface-visible defects to corresponding GPR segments, enabling efficient large-scale annotation in a real-world dataset collected on a highway section under operation. In addition to the dataset contribution, we propose a specialised 3D Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) architecture incorporating residual connections, mixed convolutional kernel sizes, and both depthwise and channelwise attention mechanisms to enhance feature representation and defect classification. The model is evaluated on binary classification tasks for detecting patch and crack defects in pavement structures. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed network outperforms baseline architectures across multiple evaluation metrics. Ablation studies further confirm the effectiveness of the designed architectural components. This work contributes a scalable and practical method for real-world dataset generation, along with a novel deep learning framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [Towards Reversible Forgetting: Managing Obsolete Knowledge in Continual Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18177)
*Nilutpaul Sarker Yash,Tirtho Roy,Ushashi Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可逆遗忘"概念框架，用于企业AI智能体在非平稳环境中管理过时知识。与传统持续学习强调保留知识不同，该框架引入主动（active）、休眠（dormant）和退役（retired）三种记忆状态，并通过迟滞可逆记忆控制器实现基于非对称阈值的状态转换、影子模式测试和策略门控的撤除机制，在减少过时信息负面影响的同时保留了知识可恢复的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习将遗忘视为失败，但企业AI智能体运行于非平稳环境中（客户、政策、工具、工作流、法规、市场条件持续变化），不加区分地保留知识会让过时信息影响决策，产生负迁移和运营风险。因此需要一种既能减轻过时信息影响又不至于永久丢失知识的记忆管理机制。

Method: 提出可逆遗忘框架，包含三个操作记忆状态：主动、休眠和退役，以及一个可恢复休眠知识的再激活转换。以迟滞可逆记忆控制器实例化该框架，其核心机制包括：累积相关性证据、使用非对称阈值防止状态振荡、在影子模式下测试再激活、通过策略门控退役。

Result: 该框架能减少过时信息对决策的影响，同时避免将暂时抑制与永久擦除相混淆。以金融领域为例：在某个市场制度下有用的知识可能在另一制度下有害，但当相似条件重现时可重新获得相关性。

Conclusion: 可逆遗忘为企业AI智能体提供了一种比传统持续学习更完整的记忆管理范式，它认同遗忘在非平稳环境中的合理性，但强调遗忘应当是可逆的、可控的，而非不可逆的删除。

Abstract: Continual learning has traditionally treated forgetting as a failure, emphasizing preservation of previously acquired knowledge as environments evolve. We argue that this objective is incomplete for enterprise AI agents operating in non-stationary environments, where customers, policies, tools, workflows, regulations, and market conditions change over time. Indiscriminate retention can allow obsolete knowledge to influence decisions, creating negative transfer and operational risk. We therefore propose reversible forgetting: a conceptual framework with three operational memory states: active, dormant, and retired, and a reactivation transition that can restore dormant knowledge when its relevance returns. We instantiate the framework as a Hysteretic Reversible Memory Controller that accumulates relevance evidence, uses asymmetric thresholds to prevent state oscillation, tests reactivation in shadow mode, and gates retirement through policy. The framework reduces the influence of obsolete information without conflating temporary suppression with permanent erasure. Finance illustrates the idea: knowledge useful under one market regime may become harmful under another yet regain relevance when similar conditions recur.

</details>


### [84] [Accelerating Visual On-Policy Distillation with Batched Speculative Jacobi Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18183)
*Bingqi Shan,Zhehao Yu,Kenhong Lin,Baoquan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HB-SJD是一种批量的推测性Jacobi解码（SJD）回放后端，用于视觉在线策略蒸馏（OPD），通过让每张图像独立推进解码进度、批量前向验证，并在图像完成时在Full和Compact执行之间切换，大幅减少回放和端到端训练时间，同时保持蒸馏学生的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通过从当前学生生成的轨迹中学习来训练紧凑的视觉自回归模型，但在线回放仍然逐token使用自回归解码生成，给每个在线策略训练步骤增加了大量成本。尽管推测性Jacobi解码（SJD）可以在无辅助草稿模型的情况下并行处理多个token，但原始方法仅设计用于单序列推理，无法直接用于批量训练场景。

Method: 提出了HB-SJD，一个批量SJD回放后端。其核心思想是：允许每张图像根据自身解码进展独立推进，同时将处于不同序列位置的图像在批量模型前向中进行验证。当图像完成解码时，HB-SJD在Full和Compact两种执行模式之间切换，以降低后续回放轮次的成本。HB-SJD仅替换学生回放后端，不改变教师模型、蒸馏目标和优化过程。

Result: 基于LlamaGen的实验表明，HB-SJD大幅减少了回放时间和端到端训练时间，同时保持了蒸馏学生的生成质量。

Conclusion: HB-SJD通过高效的批量SJD回放后端，在不影响蒸馏质量的前提下显著加速了视觉在线策略蒸馏的训练过程，为紧凑视觉自回归模型的训练提供了实用的加速方案。

Abstract: Visual on-policy distillation (OPD) improves the training of compact visual autoregressive models by learning from trajectories generated by the current student. However, these online rollouts are still produced token by token with autoregressive decoding, which adds substantial cost to every on-policy training step. Speculative Jacobi Decoding (SJD) provides an alternative because it can process multiple tokens in parallel without an auxiliary draft model, but the original method is designed for single-sequence inference. We introduce HB-SJD, a batched SJD rollout backend for visual OPD. HB-SJD allows each image to advance independently according to its own decoding progress, while images at different sequence positions are still verified in batched model forwards. As images finish, HB-SJD switches between Full and Compact execution to reduce the cost of later rollout rounds. HB-SJD only replaces the student rollout backend and leaves the teacher, distillation objective, and optimization procedure unchanged. Experiments with LlamaGen show that HB-SJD substantially reduces rollout and end-to-end training time while preserving the generation quality of the distilled student.

</details>


### [85] [SIGMA: Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive Control for Robust, Dependable Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18263)
*Pratham Payra,Jagadish B,Tanmay Sen,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIGMA是一个结合大语言模型的强化学习交通信号控制框架，通过自然语言指令将紧急车辆优先级转化为多目标actor-critic控制器的优先向量，利用旋转增强和离线到在线学习提升跨路口泛化能力，在SUMO仿真中优于传统控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在交通信号控制中存在三个主要缺陷：(1) 固定目标函数，无法适应动态变化的需求（如紧急车辆优先权）；(2) 忽略紧急车辆优先级实时调整的需求；(3) 无法在几何相似的交叉路口之间泛化。此外，手动设计奖励函数成本高昂且难以覆盖多目标权衡。

Method: SIGMA框架包含三个核心组件：(1) LLM模块将自然语言紧急命令转换为多目标actor-critic控制器的优先级向量，免去手动奖励工程；(2) 旋转增强技术实现方向不变学习，提升在四叉路口间的可迁移性；(3) 离线到在线学习策略提供稳定初始化并逐步适应交通变化。同时定义了可靠性属性（紧急服务水平、LLM故障下的优雅降级、需求敏感性），并通过bootstrap统计进行验证。

Result: 在SUMO上对印度Kolkata四个城市路口进行评估，与固定时间、感应式(actuated)和DQN控制器对比，SIGMA在平均/紧急等待时间、队长和吞吐量方面均表现更优。消融实验确认了其对组件故障和几何旋转的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SIGMA提供了一种可靠的语言引导多目标交通控制系统，具备统计可靠性保证，能够同时处理吞吐量、延迟公平性、信号稳定性和紧急车辆优先等多目标权衡，并通过组件故障下的优雅降级确保系统可靠性。

Abstract: Traffic signal control is a complex sequential decision-making problem requiring real-time adaptation and trade-offs among throughput, delay fairness, signal stability, and emergency vehicle priority. Existing RL methods often fix objectives, ignore dynamic priority changes, and fail to generalize across geometrically similar intersections.We propose SIGMA (Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive traffic control), an RL framework enhanced with a large language model (LLM) for adaptive objective tuning and orientation-invariant learning. SIGMA converts natural-language emergency commands into priority vectors for a multi-objective actor-critic controller, avoiding manual reward engineering. Rotational augmentation improves transferability across four-way intersections, while offline-to-online learning ensures stable initialization and gradual adaptation to changing traffic.We define reliability properties covering emergency service levels, graceful degradation under LLM failures, and demand sensitivity, validated via bootstrap statistics. Evaluated in SUMO on four Kolkata-based urban intersections against fixed-time, actuated, and DQN controllers, SIGMA reduces average/emergency waiting times and queue lengths, and boosts throughput. Ablation studies confirm robustness to component failures and geometric rotations. Overall, SIGMA offers a reliable, language-guided, multi-objective traffic control system with statistical reliability assurance.

</details>


### [86] [H$^2$EDL: Hyper Evidential Deep Learning for Hierarchical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18185)
*Yuanye Liu,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出H²EDL模型，利用分类法（taxonomy）自身作为超域（hyperdomain），在每个分支节点上放置一个局部Dirichlet意见，从而在线性规模的焦点族上推导出闭式合成的复合质量，统一了层次分类与证据不确定性表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度识别常涉及层次标签空间，模型可能对粗粒度概念有信心但对子类不确定。现有方法各有局限：扁平证据分类器只在叶帧上用单一空洞度表示总无知，层次分类器则传播点概率而无证据概念。超意见可统一二者，但一般形式随标签数指数增长，现有超证据网络要么需要训练数据提供复合标签，要么从非结构化权重模式读取，缺乏原则性的复合焦点选择依据。

Method: 观察到分类法本身即缺失的超域：其子树和叶单例构成线性大小的焦点族。在每个分支节点放置一个局部Dirichlet意见，通过闭式形式诱导出所有复合质量。H²EDL使用同一组参数实现两种互补解释：预测视角下作为层次分类器保持标签树各层级一致性；概率视角下定义有效的树结构超意见，节点质量表示到达该节点但不足以进一步特化到后代的信念。

Result: 在FGVC-Aircraft和DERM12345数据集上，H²EDL相较于交叉熵基线将校准误差降低约一半，且该改进在更深层次和更大训练预算下更为显著。

Conclusion: H²EDL通过将分类法结构嵌入超意见框架，以线性复杂度实现了对层次标签空间中结构化不确定性的原则性表示，在保持层次一致性的同时显著改善校准性能。

Abstract: Fine-grained recognition often involves hierarchical label spaces, where a model may be confident about a coarse semantic concept while remaining uncertain among its descendant classes. Such structured ambiguity requires uncertainty representations that capture both fine-grained classes and intermediate concepts. However, existing tools each capture only half of it: flat evidential classifiers quantify total ignorance with a single vacuity on the leaf frame, and hierarchical classifiers propagate point probabilities with no notion of evidence. Hyper-opinions would unify the two, but their general form is exponential in the label count, and existing hyper-evidential networks either require composite labels to be supplied in the training data or read them off an unstructured weight pattern, with no principled notion of which composites deserve mass. We observe that the taxonomy itself is the missing hyperdomain. Its subtrees and leaf singletons form a linear-size focal family, and one local Dirichlet opinion per branching node induces every composite mass in closed form. The resulting model, H$^2$EDL, can be interpreted in two complementary ways using the same set of parameters. From a prediction perspective, it functions as a hierarchical classifier that preserves consistency across different levels of the label tree. From a probabilistic perspective, it defines a valid tree-structured hyper-opinion, where the mass assigned to each node represents the belief that reaches that node but does not provide sufficient confidence to further specialize into its descendants. On FGVC-Aircraft and DERM12345, H$^2$EDL reduces calibration error by approximately half compared with cross-entropy baselines, with the improvement becoming more pronounced at deeper hierarchy levels and under larger training budgets.

</details>


### [87] [ProxyGuard: Direct Reliability Inference for Randomized Data Release Mechanisms with Shared Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18643)
*Dipesh Tharu Mahato,Pramod Dhungana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProxyGuard框架，用于在科研中选择代理数据集（多版本、变换或种子）时控制两类错误：无效发布被误认为充分（假阳性）以及充分生成器被低估（假阴性）。通过预设的有界风险和密封目标集，ProxyGuard在"命名发布模式"下校正多重检验并认证特定发布，在"直接共享目标模式"下评估独立机制抽取、对其有利得分率进行下界估计，并减去无效发布贡献的有利得分上界。该方法在目标条件化下利用得分独立性获得有限样本的机制可靠性保证，无需独立目标批次或对发布级p值依赖性作假设。实验显示，直接模式在可靠性0.95下将功效从5.6%提升至64.2%，而命名模式在高信号证据下表现更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者在众多数据集发布、变换或随机种子中常需选择代理数据集，搜索过程可能使无效发布显得充分，而单个充分发布也无法证明其生成机制的可靠性。现有方法缺乏对两类错误同时控制的机制，亟需一个既能校正多重检验又能提供机制级可靠性保证的框架。

Method: ProxyGuard提出两种模式：(1)命名发布模式，校正多重性并认证特定发布；(2)直接共享目标模式，评估共同目标上的独立机制抽取，对有利得分率进行下界估计（mean-only惩罚被证明是尖锐的），并减去无效发布贡献的有利得分上界。该方法证明在目标条件化下发布得分独立，获得有限样本的机制可靠性保证，并推导了具有加性目标浓度的平滑得分证书。

Result: 在注册的三需求研究中，直接模式在可靠性0.95时将功效从5.6%提升至64.2%；命名模式在强信号证据下保持更强。前瞻性审计覆盖了全流水线Rice-TVAE（每次抽取都重训练）以及非表格文本机制。

Conclusion: ProxyGuard通过有界风险和密封目标集，同时控制多重选择下的两类错误，为代理数据集选择提供统计严谨的机制可靠性认证框架。直接模式适合中等信号场景以提升功效，命名模式适合高信号场景以保持认证强度，两种模式互补覆盖不同应用需求。

Abstract: Researchers often choose a proxy dataset from many releases, transformations, or seeds. Search can make an invalid release appear adequate, while one adequate release does not establish that its generator is reliable. ProxyGuard controls both errors using prespecified bounded risks and a sealed target set. Named-release mode corrects for multiplicity and certifies specific releases. Direct shared-target mode evaluates independent mechanism draws on a common target, lower-bounds their favorable-score rate, and subtracts a bound on favorable scores contributed by invalid releases. Conditional on the target, release scores are independent, yielding a finite-sample mechanism-reliability guarantee without independent target batches or assumptions on release-level $p$-value dependence. We show that the mean-only penalty is sharp and derive a smooth-score certificate with additive target concentration. In a registered three-requirement study, direct mode raises power from 5.6\% to 64.2\% at reliability 0.95, while named mode remains stronger under high-signal evidence. Prospective audits span full-pipeline Rice--TVAE, which retrains on every draw, and a non-tabular text mechanism.

</details>


### [88] [What Can Artificial Intelligence Learn from Medicine? Generative Analogies and Reliable Machine Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18186)
*Emanuele Ratti,Lena Zuchowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Hesse的类比理论，将临床转化过程与机器学习系统构建过程视为一种生成性类比，从而为ML的认识论和方法论辩护找到依据，并提出一种新的ML可靠性主义的认识论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在医学中已广泛应用，但其认识论和方法论基础尚未明确建立。文献中虽有将医学与ML类比的观点，但往往只是笼统提及，缺乏对类比本质和转化辩护具体内容的精确刻画。

Method: 借助Hesse的分析工具，将临床转化与ML系统构建之间的平行关系特征化为一种"生成性类比"；从可靠主义（reliabilist）角度诠释临床转化的认识论与方法论辩护，并以此推演出一种新型的ML可靠性主义。

Result: 论文识别了临床转化中通常仅被笼统提及的认识论和方法论辩护，展示了这些辩护如何以类比方式适用于ML语境；提出一种区别于现有AI哲学中可靠性主义、但与之兼容的新型ML可靠性主义。

Conclusion: 临床转化与ML系统构建之间的类比不仅是启发性的，更是一种具有认识论深度的生成性类比；基于可靠主义诠释可以为一个自洽且可行的ML认识论辩护框架奠定基础。

Abstract: In the past few years, machine learning (ML) has been widely (and to an extent, successfully) implemented in medicine. However, uncertainties surrounding ML have made it difficult to establish the bases of its epistemic and methodological warrants. In the literature, a parallel has been drawn between medicine and ML, suggesting that we should model epistemic and methodological standards for ML on the standards of clinical translation. By developing tools from Hesse work, we characterise the nature of this parallel as a generative analogy between the process of clinical translation and the process of building ML systems. We identify more precisely the epistemic and methodological warrants of clinical translation that are typically only mentioned when appealing to the analogy, and we show in which sense such warrants apply analogically to the context of ML. In particular, we interpret warrants of clinical translation in reliabilist terms, and we show how this can inform a new form of ML reliabilism, which is distinct from (though compatible with) existing reliabilist accounts in philosophy of AI.

</details>


### [89] [A Unifying Relational Perspective on Expressive Lottery Tickets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18819)
*Lorenz Kummer,Samir Moustafa,Anatol Ehrlich,Franka Bause,Marco Nennstiel,Przemysław Andrzej Wałȩga,Nils Morten Kriege*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了关系型图神经网络（RGNN）和时序图神经网络（TGNN）的强表达性彩票假设（SELTH）的概率版本，证明充分参数化的RGNN包含保持1-RWL表达性的稀疏子网络，并下界了随机剪枝获得此类子网络的概率，同时将TGNN和跨图消息传递方案转化为RGNN形式以继承这些保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GNN被广泛使用，但参数稀疏性如何影响关系型（RGNN）和时序型（TGNN）变体的表达性尚未被充分理解。已有的SELTH仅针对静态图上的WL表达性，需要将其推广到多关系和时间域，从而为稀疏化GNN的可靠性提供理论基础。

Method: 作者将静态图上的SELTH存在性结果推广为多关系和时序域上的概率性陈述，通过关系型WL（RWL）进行形式化。他们证明了充分参数化的RGNN包含保持1-RWL表达性的稀疏子网络，并推导了随机剪枝获得此类子网络的概率下界。同时证明常见的TGNN和跨图消息传递方案可转化为等价的RGNN形式从而继承这些保证，并建立了稀疏RGNN表达性与常见更新机制下优化行为之间的联系。

Result: 实验验证了推导的界，将其与合成数据上的经验概率进行比较，并研究了预训练表达性如何与时序和分子基准上的优化及预测质量指标相关联。

Conclusion: 本文为稀疏化RGNN和TGNN的表达性提供了理论保证，建立了稀疏性与优化行为之间的联系，并实证验证了理论结果在时序和分子任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are widely used, but how parameter sparsity affects the expressivity of relational (RGNNs) and temporal (TGNNs) variants is poorly understood. The Strong Expressive Lottery Ticket Hypothesis (SELTH) posits the existence of sparse GNNs that preserve Weisfeiler-Leman (WL) expressivity on static graphs. We generalize this existence result to a probabilistic statement for multi-relational and temporal domains via the relational WL (RWL). We prove that sufficiently parameterized RGNNs contain sparse subnetworks that maintain 1-RWL expressivity and derive a lower bound on the probability that a random pruning yields such a subnetwork. We show that common TGNNs and cross-graph message passing schemes admit RGNN reformulations such that they inherit these guarantees and, moreover, that the expressivity of a sparse RGNN is connected to its optimization behavior under common update regimes. Experiments instantiate the bound, compare it to empirical probabilities on synthetic data, and study how pre-training expressivity relates to optimization and prediction quality metrics on temporal and molecular benchmarks.

</details>


### [90] [A systematic review of machine learning techniques to address diagnosis and treatment of autism: challenges and opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18188)
*Rafael Muñoz-Terol,Jesús Peral,Sandra Amador,David Gil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本系统综述分析了2017至2023年间55项关于机器学习（ML）在自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）中应用的研究，发现监督学习方法占主导地位，深度学习应用正随数据可用性增加而扩展，并指出未来需通过多模态数据整合和跨学科合作来提升诊断准确性与治疗成效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD的病因尚不明确，识别相关特征和隐藏相关性对于早期诊断至关重要，因此有必要系统梳理近年来机器学习技术在ASD研究中的应用现状，以指导未来研究方向。

Method: 对2017至2023年间发表的55项关于机器学习应用于ASD的研究进行系统性综述，重点考察技术趋势、所用算法和数据集，并分析监督学习、深度学习和混合方法的演进路径。

Result: 监督学习方法在ASD诊断中占据主导地位，深度学习的角色随数据规模扩大而日益重要；混合方法（融合无监督学习、深度学习和模糊逻辑）被视为未来值得关注的新兴方向。同时识别出需整合基因与临床等多源数据、引入可穿戴设备和生物传感器等新型数据源等关键挑战与机遇。

Conclusion: 应对ASD研究中的现有挑战需要跨学科合作和面向ASD的扩充数据集；未来ML模型将通过更广泛的多模态数据整合来更全面地处理ASD的复杂性，从而实现更精准的诊断和更优的治疗效果。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) is a developmental disability characterized by challenges in social interaction and communication. As the causes of ASD remain unclear, identifying relevant features and hidden correlations is crucial for early diagnosis. This systematic review evaluates 55 studies from 2017 to 2023 on the application of machine learning (ML) techniques to ASD. The primary objective is to examine recent ML applications in ASD research, identifying trends, techniques, and datasets that enhance diagnosis and treatment. Supervised learning methods dominate, as they align well with ASD diagnostic needs; however, the role of deep learning is expanding with greater data availability. Emerging techniques based on hybrid methods, where unsupervised, deep learning, and fuzzy logic could be included, will be interesting to observe in the future. The review highlights key challenges and opportunities, particularly the need for models that can integrate complex data -such as genetic and clinical information- to improve diagnostic accuracy and treatment outcomes. Additionally, incorporating innovative data sources, like wearable devices and biometric sensors, could enable continuous and non-intrusive monitoring, providing a more holistic understanding of ASD. Findings emphasize that addressing current challenges requires interdisciplinary collaboration and expanded datasets tailored to ASD. Future ML models will benefit from broader multimodal data integration, enabling researchers to more comprehensively address the complexities of ASD.

</details>


### [91] [GEAR: Generative Expansion and Real Anchoring for Two-Stage Distillation of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18849)
*Qi Qin,Jiajie Zhu,Dali Chen,Yuzhao Zhang,Jia-Xing Han,Yu Su,Peng Zhang,Ying Yan,Yifan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEAR是一种两阶段蒸馏框架，将表格基础模型（TFM）蒸馏为轻量级MLP或树模型，部署在商用CPU上。阶段1使用合成协变量作为教师查询位置，训练学生在软TFM目标上扩展覆盖范围；阶段2使用真实标签和袋外教师预测重新锚定学生到目标分布，避免自标记泄漏。实验表明GEAR显著提升性能、稳定性和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）虽通过上下文学习实现强性能，但其依赖上下文的推理方式导致高延迟和内存开销，阻碍大规模部署。需要一种在不牺牲性能的前提下将TFM压缩为轻量级可部署模型的方法。

Method: 提出GEAR（Generative Expansion and Real Anchoring）模块化两阶段框架：阶段1中，利用合成协变量仅作为教师查询位置，训练学生模型在软TFM目标上进行生成式扩展，超越已观测行的覆盖范围；阶段2中，利用真实标签和袋外教师预测重新锚定学生对目标分布的学习，避免自标签泄漏。同时推导了一个风险证书来刻画生成查询量与生成器保真度之间的权衡关系。

Result: 在TALENT和TabArena上的实验表明：两阶段MLP在二分类任务上比监督MLP高1.81-2.00 AUC点，多分类任务高1.19-1.35点；对比仅真实数据蒸馏分别多出1.76-2.19和2.09-2.40点增益。二分类增益可迁移到LightGBM和XGBoost，三个学生族平均AUC均优于最强非TFM基线CatBoost。消融实验验证了收益超越更长训练或替代热启动，分阶段优化比混合优化更稳定。推理时延中位数降低57-2866倍，峰值预测内存降低1.9-3.3倍，同时保持优于匹配监督基线的高AUC。

Conclusion: GEAR通过扩展生成+真实锚定的两阶段蒸馏策略，成功将TFM的知识高效迁移到轻量级模型，在保持甚至提升预测性能的同时大幅降低推理成本和内存占用，为表格基础模型的大规模部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) achieve strong performance through in-context learning, but context-dependent inference imposes substantial latency and memory costs, hindering large-scale deployment. We propose GEAR (\emph{Generative Expansion and Real Anchoring}), a modular two-stage framework that distills TFMs into lightweight MLP or tree-based predictors that can be deployed on commodity CPUs. Stage 1 uses synthetic covariates solely as teacher-query locations and trains the student on soft TFM targets, expanding coverage beyond observed rows. Stage 2 re-anchors the student to the target distribution using real labels and out-of-fold teacher predictions, whitch avoids self-labeling leakage. We further derive a risk certificate characterizing the trade-off between generated-query volume and generator fidelity. Experiments on TALENT and TabArena demonstrate the broad applicability of GEAR. Two-stage MLPs outperform supervised MLPs by 1.81--2.00 AUC points on binary tasks and 1.19--1.35 points on multiclass tasks, with additional gains over real-data-only distillation of 1.76--2.19 and 2.09--2.40 points, respectively. On binary tasks, the gains also transfer to LightGBM and XGBoost, and all three student families outperform CatBoost, the strongest non-TFM baseline, in mean AUC. Ablations show gains beyond longer training or alternative warm starts, greater stability from staged than mixed optimization, and generator-dependent diminishing returns as query volume increases. Finally, GEAR reduces median inference time by 57--2866 times and peak prediction memory by 1.9--3.3 times, while retaining higher AUC than matched supervised baselines.

</details>


### [92] [Safe Domain Adaptation for Physics: Overcoming Nuisances, Label Shifts, and Simulation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18190)
*Ivan Kharuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究物理领域中域适应（Domain Adaptation）的失效场景：当模拟与实验数据在目标物理量分布上本身不一致时，标准对抗性域适应会错误地对齐频谱分布，引入受模拟先验支配的系统偏差。作者提出“自适应域适应”（adaptive domain adaptation），通过重加权模拟事件使域适应仅聚焦于真实的物理失配，并提供无标签模型选择规则选取近似最优操作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统域适应假设两个域仅存在“干扰性”差异，且感兴趣的目标量在两个域中分布相同。但在物理学中，这两个假设均不成立：模拟可能在物理上就是错误的，且目标量（如能量谱、红移分布）的分布本身就是待测量对象。因此直接套用标准域适应会导致有偏结果，需要研究失配场景下域适应失效的机理并提出修正方案。

Method: 作者在玩具大气簇射（air-shower）基准上构建可控实验，可独立或组合切换三种效应：检测器响应的干扰项、物理模拟偏移、能量谱偏移。标准对抗性域适应用于处理条件偏移；提出“自适应域适应”（adaptive domain adaptation），通过对模拟事件进行重加权，使域适应专注于真实的物理失配而非谱分布差异。同时提出一种无需标签的模型选择准则，用于选取接近最优的操作点。

Result: 证明了标准对抗性域适应在目标谱分布不一致时会将两个域的频谱强行对齐，从而用一个锚定于模拟先验的系统偏差替代原来的无约束偏差；所提出的自适应域适应方法能够准确聚焦于物理失配，并借助无标签模型选择规则获得近最优性能。

Conclusion: 物理应用中域适应需要对“干扰差异”与“物理差异”进行显式区分；仅依赖对抗对齐而不考虑目标量分布本身的不同，会引入由模拟先验主导的偏差。自适应重加权与无标签模型选择为解决此类问题提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Domain adaptation is widely used to make neural networks trained on simulations applicable to experimental data. Its premise is that the two domains differ only in nuisances, and that the quantity of interest is distributed identically in both. In physics neither assumption holds: simulations can be wrong about the physics, and the distribution of the target quantity - an energy spectrum, a redshift distribution - is often the measurement itself. We study the consequences of such mismatches on a toy air-shower benchmark in which a detector-response nuisance, a physical simulation shift, and an energy-spectrum shift can be switched on separately or together. Standard adversarial adaptation handles the conditional shifts, but once the two spectra differ it aligns them, replacing an uncontrolled bias by one anchored on the simulation prior. We present adaptive domain adaptation, which reweights the simulated events so as to focus domain adaptation on the genuine physical mismatch alone. Since the predicted spectrum depends on model training configuration, we provide a label-free model selection rule for selecting the near-the-best operation point.

</details>


### [93] [ChiroEcho: extending automated bat vocalisation classification beyond the learned taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18191)
*Burooj Ghani,Welmoed Eversteijn,Milan van Hirtum,Juan Sebastián Cañas,Vincent J. Kalkman,Dan Stowell,A. Leonie Baier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种深度学习框架，联合预测蝙蝠的物种和属级别，并在推理时将属预测与地理物种分布相结合。当预测属在某一地区仅对应单一物种时，该框架可解析出训练集分类体系之外的物种，从而将地理信息用于扩展（而非约束）分类器的有效分类范围。在35个欧洲蝙蝠物种数据上验证，地理扩展将操作覆盖率从73%（35种）提升至85%（41种），覆盖48种原生欧洲蝙蝠中的41种，据称为目前欧洲自动蝙蝠分类中最广泛的操作覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蝙蝠是欧洲受保护的生态系统健康指示物种，可靠种群监测是保护优先事项。但蝙蝠夜间隐蔽生活，被动声学监测成为必需，而回声定位叫声随行为和环境影响较大且物种间重叠，导致自动识别困难。现有方法面临稀疏物种性能估计不稳定、分类器无法识别训练体系外物种等问题。

Method: 构建深度学习框架，联合预测物种和属两个层级；推理时将属预测与地理物种分布结合，实现空间约束下的物种解析。该框架重新定义地理信息的作用——用于扩展而非约束分类器的有效分类体系。通过涵盖35种欧洲蝙蝠的录音进行闭集分类评估、稀疏物种性能估计不稳定性分析以及受控留出验证实验。

Result: 闭集分类有效；稀疏物种分析显示有限评估数据可能掩盖物种级真实性能；留出实验证明属预测加位置可恢复物种头无法获得的标签。地理扩展将操作覆盖率从35种提升至41种（覆盖48种原生欧洲蝙蝠的73%增至85%），据称为已报道的欧洲自动化蝙蝠分类中最广泛的操作覆盖。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了通过粗粒度预测与透明外部约束（地理分布）相结合来解析未见的细粒度类别的原理可行性，为自动化蝙蝠分类提供了更广泛的操作覆盖，也为其他领域类似问题提供了通用方法论参考。

Abstract: Bats are key indicators of ecosystem health and are protected throughout Europe, making reliable population monitoring a conservation priority. Their cryptic nocturnal lifestyle makes passive acoustic monitoring essential, yet automated identification remains difficult as echolocation calls vary with behaviour and environment and overlap among species. We present a deep learning framework that jointly predicts species and genus and combines genus predictions with geographic species distributions at inference. When only one species of a predicted genus occurs in a region, the framework can resolve species absent from the learned taxonomy. This reframes geographic information as a means of extending, rather than constraining, a classifier's effective taxonomy. Using recordings spanning 35 European bat species, we evaluate closed-set classification, examine the instability of performance estimates for sparsely represented species, and conduct a controlled held-out proof-of-principle experiment. The rare-species analysis shows how limited evaluation data can obscure species-level performance, while the held-out experiment shows that genus predictions and location can recover labels unavailable to the species head. Geographic resolution extends operational coverage from 35 to 41 of the 48 native European bat species, increasing coverage from 73% to 85%. To our knowledge, this is the broadest operational coverage reported for automated European bat classification. More broadly, the bat framework provides proof of principle for resolving unseen fine-grained classes by combining coarse predictions with transparent external constraints.

</details>


### [94] [Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning for Controlled Hawkes Jump-Diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19151)
*Tomasz R. Bielecki,Thibaut Mastrolia,Haoze Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非马尔可夫框架下多元Hawkes驱动随机微分方程的最优控制问题，提出了一种有限维马尔可夫化近似方法（用混合指数核逼近Hawkes核），并在此基础上开发了连续时间确定性策略梯度学习算法（Hawkes-CT DDPG）。该方法仅需观测事件时间、SDE解实现和衰减滤波器即可进行无模型优化，且通过实验与离散时间强化学习在指数核、Erlang核和幂律核下进行了对比验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元Hawkes过程驱动的随机控制问题因Hawkes强度的路径依赖记忆特性，在一般核函数下不满足经典随机控制理论的马尔可夫框架，现有方法无法直接处理这种非马尔可夫设定下的最优控制问题。

Method: 1) 提出用混合指数核的有限维马尔可夫化程序近似多元Hawkes过程，并证明Hawkes过程、其强度及原始问题值函数的收敛性；2) 在马尔可夫化近似上建立连续时间确定性策略梯度学习算法（Hawkes-CT DDPG）；3) 设计无模型算法，仅通过观测事件时间、SDE解和衰减滤波器求解，无需知晓Hawkes核系数。

Result: 该算法在三种不同类型的核（简单指数、Erlang和幂律核）下，与离散时间强化学习技术进行了比较实验，验证了所提连续时间方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文通过马尔可夫化近似与连续时间强化学习的结合，为非马尔可夫Hawkes驱动随机控制问题提供了一种可行的无模型求解方案，拓展了随机控制与强化学习在该类路径依赖问题中的应用边界。

Abstract: We study stochastic control of multivariate Hawkes-driven stochastic differential equations with machine learning algorithms in a non-Markovian setting. Due to the path dependence of the memory of the Hawkes intensity, this problem does not fall within classical stochastic control theory outside particular Markovian kernels. We first develop a finite-dimensional Markovianization procedure and algorithm to approximate multivariate Hawkes processes with mixtures of exponential kernels. We prove the convergence of the Markovianized approximation of the Hawkes process, its intensity, and the value of the problem to the original non-Markovian processes and the value of the primal problem. We then formulate continuous-time deterministic policy gradient learning on the Markovianized approximation of the problem, called Hawkes-CT DDPG. We propose a model-free algorithm to solve the non-Markovian Hawkes-driven optimization by observing only the event times of the process, the realization of the solution to the SDE, and a chosen set of decay filters, while the Hawkes kernel coefficients remain unknown. We compare our continuous time reinforcement learning Hawkes-CT DDPG method with discrete time reinforcement learning techniques under three different types of kernels: simple exponential, Erlang, and power-law kernels.

</details>


### [95] [Think Shallow, Solve Deep: Controlling Recurrent Dynamics for Reliable Test-Time Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18222)
*Ivan Viakhirev,Kirill Borodin,Amirah Almutairi,Serguei Barannikov,Maxim Abramov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了递归深度推理器（recurrent-depth reasoners）在测试时增加迭代次数对性能的影响，提出通过估计算子的有限时间动力学状态（settling/marginal/drifting）来预测深度行为，并给出深度安全（depth-safety）的充分条件；实验表明固定点目标可使算子进入settling状态，从而在更难的未见实例上获得准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归深度推理器希望通过在测试时延长迭代次数来解决更困难的问题，但额外迭代可能提升、保持或降低答案质量，这种不确定的行为缺乏可预测的理论依据，迫切需要一种可度量的属性来预先判断深度行为的好坏。

Method: 作者将训练后的算子的有限时间动力学状态分为三类（settling、marginal、drifting），并给出深度安全的充分条件：当算子每步位移相对于解码器边界足够小时，进一步迭代不再改变解码答案。通过单一终端固定点目标（terminal fixed-point objective）来调控动力学状态，并提供四个可用于测试时深度的操作准则来分类失败模式。

Result: 在每难度层级仅800个未经增强样本的算法任务上，settling算子在增加深度时不会退化，且在部分任务上将额外深度转化为更困难未见实例上的更高准确率（Sudoku从0.19提升到0.34，超出训练范围）。移除固定点目标会诱导drift并消除增益，而将其加入通用递归可产生深度安全的进位传播外推。作为一致性检验，Huginn-3.5B落入非settling族。

Conclusion: 有限时间动力学状态是递归深度推理器测试时深度行为的关键可度量指标；固定点目标通过驱动算子进入settling状态将额外深度转化为泛化增益，为设计深度安全的推理器提供了操作化准则。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth reasoners aim to solve harder problems by iterating their update longer at test time, but additional iterations can improve, preserve, or degrade an answer. We show that a measurable property of the trained operator, its finite-time dynamical regime (estimated as settling, marginal, or drifting), indicates which of these occurs. We give a sufficient condition for depth-safety: once an operator's per-step displacement is small relative to the decoder margin, the decoded answer cannot change under further iterations. Empirically, on algorithmic tasks trained from $800$ unaugmented examples per difficulty tier, settling operators do not degrade with added depth, and on some tasks convert it into higher accuracy on harder unseen instances (Sudoku, $0.19$ to $0.34$ past the training horizon). A single terminal fixed-point objective moves the regime and the depth behavior together: removing it induces drift and removes the gains, and adding it to a generic recurrence yields depth-safe extrapolation on carry propagation. We give four operational criteria for useful test-time depth, use them to catalogue failure modes, and, as a consistency check, apply the same measurements to Huginn-3.5B, which falls in the non-settling family.

</details>


### [96] [Allocating Recurrent Compute in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18230)
*Ruhai Lin,Yiyang Guo,Rui-Jie Zhu,Hao Ye,Jason K. Eshraghian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MixerLoop架构，仅循环重复Gated DeltaNet mixer（注意力混合器）而非整个层堆栈，将密集FFN只执行一次。在15M和110M参数规模下，MixerLoop在CORE基准上不逊色或接近全块循环（FullLoop），同时将循环骨干投影的FLOPs降低45.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环语言模型（如looped transformers）通常重复整层堆栈，但mixer（如注意力）和FFN执行不同操作且成本不同。作者提出一个更精细的问题：究竟应该循环什么？通过引入Iterative Transport Rank (ITR)和marginal ITR来量化重复应用带来的非冗余跨位置影响，从而指导循环结构的设计。

Method: 1) 理论框架：将循环视为状态更新的重复组合，用ITR描述累积影响轨迹，用marginal ITR描述后续应用的非冗余贡献；2) 提出MixerLoop架构：重复Gated DeltaNet mixer多次，但FFN只应用一次；3) 与无循环和全块循环（FullLoop）在15M和110M参数下同数据、同初始化、同架构对比；4) 使用有限上下文偏移干预（finite context-off intervention）测试后续mixer应用是否产生不同、非可忽略且有益的最终读出变化。

Result: MixerLoop在15M参数下CORE聚合指标超过FullLoop；在110M下保留了FullLoop 41.5%的CORE提升，同时将循环骨干投影FLOPs降低45.9%。实验表明循环深度带来的收益可以在不重复执行密集FFN的情况下保留。

Conclusion: 循环深度（recurrent depth）的收益主要来自mixer的重复应用而非FFN的重复执行。MixerLoop在保持推理和知识操作能力的同时显著降低计算成本，为设计高效的循环语言模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Looped language models improve reasoning and knowledge manipulation by applying shared computation repeatedly. Existing systems usually repeat an entire layer stack, although a mixer and a dense feed-forward network (FFN) perform different operations and have different costs. We ask a narrower question: what should loop? We view recurrence as repeated composition of a state update and argue that an application is valuable when it exposes a new cross-position influence direction that remains observable at the task readout. Iterative Transport Rank (ITR) describes the cumulative influence trajectory; marginal ITR describes the nonredundant influence contributed by successive applications. This view motivates MixerLoop, which repeats each Gated DeltaNet mixer while applying its dense FFN once. We compare MixerLoop with no recurrence and full-block recurrence at 15M and 110M parameters under the same data, initialization, and architecture. A finite context-off intervention tests whether later mixer applications produce distinct, non-negligible, and beneficial changes at the final language-model readout. MixerLoop surpasses FullLoop on aggregate CORE at 15M and retains 41.5% of its CORE improvement at 110M while reducing recurrent-backbone projection FLOPs by 45.9%. These results show that the benefits of recurrent depth can be retained without repeatedly executing the dense FFN.

</details>


### [97] [Classifying Directional Trajectories Near Criticality in the Three-State Majority-Vote Model with Deep Belief Networks and Bidirectional GRUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18235)
*Mauricio A. Valle,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在三维多数投票模型（MV3）中，使用DBN和Bi-GRU的级联架构来区分四种动态轨迹类型（从无序接近、从有序接近、向无序离开、向有序离开）。DBN通过无监督学习将静态晶格快照编码为81维向量，而两层的Bi-GRU分类器基于DBN编码的序列实现近乎完美的四类分离，并可通过滑动窗口实时感知系统动力学状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于静态配置学习的模型无法充分捕获时间方向上的动态信息，因此需要一种能够区分不同动态轨迹类型（不仅是静态状态）的层次化架构，用于检测和分类基于主体的舆论动力学模型中的临界转变。

Method: 采用级联架构：首先用高斯-伯努利受限玻尔兹曼机输入层预训练的DBN（784→4096→225→81），以无监督方式将静态平衡样本编码为81维潜在向量；然后使用两层Bi-GRU分类器对长度为T=50的编码快照序列进行训练，在隐藏状态空间区分四种轨迹类型；最后用t-SNE可视化验证分离效果，并通过滑动窗口对连续MV3动力学进行实时应用。

Result: DBN的t-SNE分析仅显示四类轨迹的部分分离，表明静态训练模型无法完全解决方向性时间结构；而Bi-GRU在训练集和测试集上均实现了四类轨迹近乎完美的分离；滑动窗口应用展示了系统对实时动力学状态变化的感知能力。

Conclusion: 该级联层次架构为检测和分类基于智能体的舆论动力学模型中的临界转变提供了原理性方法，证明了将静态特征提取（DBN）与序列建模（Bi-GRU）相结合的架构在动态轨迹分类上的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate whether the latent representations learned by a Deep Belief Network (DBN) and a Bidirectional Gated Recurrent Unit (Bi-GRU) can discriminate among four dynamically distinct trajectory types in the three-state majority vote model (MV3): approach from disorder, approach from order, departure to disorder, and departure to order. The DBN, pre-trained in an unsupervised manner on static equilibrium samples via a Gaussian-Bernoulli Restricted Boltzmann Machine input layer and architecture $784 \to 4096 \to 225 \to 81$, encodes each lattice snapshot into an 81-dimensional latent vector. A t-SNE analysis of the DBN latent space reveals only partial separation of the four trajectory types, reflecting the fact that a model trained on static configurations cannot fully resolve directional temporal structure. A two-layer Bi-GRU classifier, trained on sequences of DBN-encoded snapshots of length $T = 50$, achieves near-perfect separation of all four trajectory types in its hidden state space, as confirmed by t-SNE visualization on both training and test sets. Furthermore, a sliding-window application of the trained Bi-GRU to continuous MV3 dynamics demonstrates its ability to sense the system's current dynamical regime in real-time. These results establish a principled hierarchical architecture for detecting and classifying critical transitions in agent-based opinion dynamics models.

</details>


### [98] [ClosureBench: A Constructive Benchmark for Compositional Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18242)
*Stefano Goria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ClosureBench，一个通过Ein张量逻辑语言程序执行实时生成实例的构造式组合图关系推理基准，避免了静态测试集的数据污染问题。评估了从15亿参数到前沿闭源模型，发现三个关键发现：1)该基准能直接测量记忆化（微调模型在见过与新鲜实例间存在19.3%准确率差距）；2)准确率随图规模和查询深度下降，且两者交互——模型误读图的自然语言描述后在错误图上推理，即使最强前沿模型也如此；3)一个4B模型通过微调生成可执行程序而非直接答案，在组合层级间保持近乎平坦的准确率（94.3%），接近前沿模型水平且token成本极低，且对Ein和Python+NetworkX两种程序目标均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图关系推理基准使用固定测试集，易受到数据污染影响，且无法区分模型是真正学会推理还是记住了训练数据中的答案。作者旨在构建一个可程序验证、按需生成实例的基准，以可靠地评估模型的组合图关系推理能力，并揭示模型在从原子推理到组合推理过程中的真实瓶颈。

Method: ClosureBench通过执行Ein张量逻辑语言程序来生成实例并计算参考答案，确保机器验证的正确性。基准包含26个任务类别，分为L1-L3三个组合层级，沿图大小、边密度和查询深度三个独立轴控制难度。评估范围从1.5B开源模型到前沿闭源模型（o3、GPT-4.1、Gemini 2.5、Claude Sonnet 4），并进行对比实验包括：微调模型在固定测试集上的记忆化测量、不同输入格式（自然语言/JSON边列表/邻接矩阵）的比较，以及4B模型生成可执行程序（Ein或Python+NetworkX）而非直接答案的训练策略。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1)在固定测试集上微调的模型在见过与新鲜实例间存在19.3个百分点准确率差距，静态基准无法揭示这一点；(2)准确率随图大小和查询深度增加而下降，两者交互作用——模型的主要瓶颈是误读图的自然语言描述然后在错误图上正确推理，此问题在所有输入格式（自然语言、JSON、邻接矩阵）中均存在；(3)4B模型通过生成可执行程序的方式在组合层级间保持几乎平坦的准确率，达到94.3%的held-out准确率，接近前沿模型且token成本大幅降低，该结果在Ein和Python+NetworkX两种程序目标上均一致。

Conclusion: ClosureBench证明了两点：一是静态基准会掩盖模型的记忆化问题，而按需生成实例的构造式基准能够直接测量；二是模型在组合图推理中的核心瓶颈在于从自然语言描述中准确构建图结构，而非后续的推理步骤，而采用程序合成（生成可执行代码）的方法可以显著缓解这一瓶颈，使小模型也接近前沿水平。这为图推理评估和训练范式提供了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce ClosureBench, a constructive benchmark for compositional graph-relational reasoning with programmatically verified ground truth. Unlike fixed-test-set benchmarks vulnerable to data contamination, ClosureBench generates instances on demand: each task's reference answer is computed by executing a program in the Ein tensor-logic language, ensuring machine-verified correctness. The benchmark spans 26 task categories at three compositional levels (L1-L3), with difficulty controlled along three independent axes: graph size, edge density, and query depth.
  We evaluate models from 1.5B open weights to frontier systems (o3, GPT-4.1, Gemini 2.5, Claude Sonnet 4) and report three findings. First, because the benchmark can always supply fresh instances, it measures memorisation directly: a model fine-tuned on a fixed test set shows a 19.3 percentage-point gap between its accuracy on seen and on fresh instances, which a static test set cannot reveal. We scope this to supervised fine-tuning on answer pairs, not pretraining contamination. Second, accuracy falls as graph size and query depth increase, and the two interact: models misread the graph from its natural-language description and then reason correctly over the wrong graph, so even the strongest frontier model degrades from atomic to compositional queries. This bottleneck is a property of the reasoning rather than the input format: it persists when the graph is given as a JSON edge list or an adjacency matrix instead of prose. Third, a 4B model fine-tuned to emit executable programs rather than answers stays nearly flat across compositional levels and approaches frontier accuracy (94.3% on held-out instances) at a fraction of the token cost. This holds for two program targets, Ein and Python+NetworkX, so it is a property of verified program synthesis rather than of one language.

</details>


### [99] [Bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18244)
*Samuel Kostousov,Abhinn Kaushik,Brokoslaw Laschowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现生物与人工神经网络的表征对齐存在不对称性，并开发了一个整合谱正则化与双向可预测性分析的计算框架，通过调控学习表征的谱几何，实现了55%的双向对齐相对改进，表明表征几何可以系统性调节双向表征对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明生物与人工神经网络之间的表征对齐是不对称的：模型表征预测神经反应的能力远强于神经反应预测模型表征的能力。这种不对称性引发了一个核心问题：表征几何是否会影响双向对齐，即是否可以通过调整表征几何来系统地改变这种不对称性。

Method: 开发了一个将谱正则化与双向可预测性分析相结合的计算框架。在自监督对比视觉模型上进行评估，通过调控学习表征的谱几何（spectral geometry）来观察其对前向可预测性（forward predictivity）和反向可预测性（reverse predictivity）的影响。

Result: 调控谱几何显著提高了反向可预测性，同时前向可预测性仅略有下降，双向可预测性实现了55%的相对提升。这些改进伴随着有效维度的降低以及共享表征子空间的重组，在中间谱指数处前向与反向可预测性变得近似对称。

Conclusion: 表征几何可以被系统地调控以调节生物与人工神经网络之间的双向表征对齐，这为理解生物-人工对齐的机制提供了新的途径，并为构建更佳的神经预测模型提供了指导。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks is asymmetric: model representations predict neural responses much better than neural responses predict model representations. This asymmetry raises the question of whether representational geometry contributes to bidirectional representational alignment. We hypothesized that steering representational geometry during training can systematically influence bidirectional alignment. To test this hypothesis, we developed a computational framework that integrates spectral regularization with bidirectional predictivity analyses. As an initial demonstration, we evaluated our framework using self-supervised contrastive vision models. Steering the spectral geometry of the learned representations substantially increased reverse predictivity with modest reductions in forward predictivity, yielding a 55% relative improvement in bidirectional predictivity. These improvements were accompanied by reduced effective dimensionality and a reorganization of the shared representational subspace, within which forward and reverse predictivity became approximately symmetric at intermediate spectral exponents. Overall, these findings demonstrate that representational geometry can be systematically steered to modulate bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks.

</details>


### [100] [Rethinking Privileged Information in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18271)
*Samyak Shrestha,Alexander Tessier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中特权参考信息对学生学习的实际贡献，发现性能提升并不能归因于学生学到了参考中的信息，而更可能是恢复了基础模型中已有的推理行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD方法在训练学生模型时使用同一模型（基于特权参考信息）产生的token级监督。已有研究表明OPSD能带来性能提升，但尚不清楚这种提升究竟是因为学生真正学到了参考信息，还是仅仅恢复了基础模型中已存在的推理能力。这一区分对于理解自蒸馏机制的本质至关重要。

Method: 使用Qwen3系列模型（1.7B至8B）在科学和数学数据集上进行OPSD实验。提出了一个分析框架，将参考信息引起的监督与教师不带参考信息提供的监督分离，测量各自与学生预测变化的一致性。同时设计对照实验，使用其他问题的解决方案作为参考，检验参考信息的特异性贡献。

Result: （1）正确参考在教师生成模式、模型规模和训练数据集上并不提供一致的性能优势；（2）学生即使没有正确参考也能提升，且在多个数学推理基准上，其他问题的解决方案有时优于正确方案；（3）学生预测与基础模型思考行为的一致性高于与参考监督的一致性，但其他问题的对照重现了大部分这种一致性；（4）更强的参考对齐并不可靠地对应更大的参考性能收益。因此性能和分布对齐无法确定特权参考信息对OPSD学习的贡献方式。

Conclusion: OPSD带来的性能提升和对齐模式不能证明学生从特权参考信息中学习了新知识；这些提升很可能主要反映学生对基础模型已有推理行为的恢复。需要新的评估方法才能真正理解OPSD中参考信息的贡献机制。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own responses using token-level supervision from the same model conditioned on privileged reference information. We investigate whether performance gains from OPSD show that the student learned the information in the reference or instead reflect recovery of reasoning behavior already present in the base model. We perform OPSD experiments on science and mathematics datasets using Qwen3 models ranging from 1.7B to 8B. Our analysis framework separates the supervision induced by the reference from the supervision provided by the teacher without the reference and measures how each aligns with changes in the student's predictions. The correct reference does not provide a consistent performance benefit across teacher generation modes, model sizes, and training datasets. Students can improve without the correct reference, and a solution from another problem can outperform the correct solution on several mathematical reasoning benchmarks. The student's predictions align more strongly with the base model's thinking behavior than with the supervision induced by the reference, but controls constructed from other problems reproduce much of both alignments. Moreover, stronger alignment attributable to the correct reference does not reliably coincide with a greater performance benefit from the reference. Performance gains and distributional alignment alone therefore cannot determine how privileged reference information contributes to student learning in OPSD.

</details>


### [101] [SingularClip: Preventing Spectral Collapse to Maintain Plasticity in Continual and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18319)
*Tyler Kastner,Nimrod De La Vega,Amir-massoud Farahmand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SingularClip的新方法，通过周期性地裁剪权重矩阵的奇异值来缓解神经网络在非平稳任务训练中出现的可塑性损失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在非平稳任务上训练时，往往会出现可塑性损失（loss of plasticity），即无法再良好地拟合新目标。现有研究对可塑性损失的来源理解仍不充分，作者发现了一个新的导致可塑性丧失的来源——训练过程中权重矩阵奇异值的各向异性不断增长，并希望通过理解这一现象并提出相应缓解策略来改善模型的持续学习能力。

Method: 作者首先从经验和理论两个层面分析了权重矩阵奇异值的各向异性增长如何导致可塑性损失。基于这一分析，提出了SingularClip算法，该算法周期性地对所有权重矩阵的奇异值进行裁剪（clipping），以控制奇异值分布的过度展开，从而维持网络的可塑性。

Result: 在一系列任务（包括持续监督学习和深度强化学习）的基准测试中，SingularClip在多个任务上均表现出优于基线方法的性能，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 权重矩阵奇异值的各向异性增长是可塑性损失的一个关键来源；SingularClip通过周期性裁剪奇异值能够有效缓解这一问题，在持续监督学习和深度强化学习任务上均取得了强于基线的表现。
